# Priority Decode body, layer and operator scoreboard

Coverage status: **partial**. Missing predictions and unmatched roles stay visible.

Current selected Body arms: **52/66 passed**. One latest completed receipt per measured source/geometry, selected in input order; each row retains its own runtime and case.

Latest attempted Body arms: **66/66 completed**, **0 failed**, **52 passed**. An earlier completed prediction remains available above when the newest attempt fails; it is not a successful new replay.

Body: **180/618 prediction arms passed**; 66 unique sources, 222 measured geometry populations, 5250 unique complete Forwards (includes deferred populations).

Invalidated prediction rows: **31**. Their measured populations remain; superseded predictions and layer errors are excluded from accuracy statistics.

Current operator strict accuracy (latest whole-source receipt plus complete native-family supplements): heavy_relative_15pct: **17/28**; micro_absolute_15us: **740/763**. Conditional references are excluded.

Operator runtime coverage (recorded Sim file-map hash → source/geometry populations): `c27d72c215c2` → 5; `20dbf57be438` → 1; `fd8238dbb993` → 5; `e78bca41dba4` → 30; `mixed_native` → 3; `d5345a2d23c0` → 7; `b8dd0b80a283` → 3. Mixed populations retain each family’s own runtime after scoped supplements; this aggregate can contain different runtimes.

Native-family supplement applications: **3**; only the complete named family is replaced, never the source’s other operator rows.

Strict event coverage: **1,431,166/7,799,454** events across 54 measured source/geometry populations. A high pass rate within this subset does not establish whole-model accuracy.

Local replay receipts with explicit comparison coverage. Body errors are measured wall-clock differences; operator priorities are nonadditive kernel activity residuals per complete Forward.

Observed small-payload / low-M effective rates and per-call / per-group service parameters are accepted modelling inputs. They use the same accuracy table; measurement origin alone never demotes a result to a conditional reference. Declared in-sample accuracy is included; held-out validation is a separate evidence dimension. Unspecified calibration provenance remains not_declared. Contract and physical boundary gaps remain separately visible.

- Rows retain their own source/runtime/geometry; independently produced receipts are not merged into a synthetic run.
- Per-forward kernel activity residual ranks work to investigate; overlap prevents interpreting its sum as body wall-clock error.
- Unscored activity is a matching/model-boundary coverage gap, not proof that Sim omitted the corresponding work.
- Layer median-of-call-P50 and exact pooled P50 are distinct populations.
